A key ingredient is a localized change-of-measure lemma that yields probability-level arm exploration guarantees, which is of independent interest. We also give an algorithm using $O(\log T)$ bits of memory and $\widetilde{O}(K)$ batches that achieves regret $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{KT})$, which nearly matches our lower bound.

</details>


### [152] [Manifold-Orthogonal Dual-spectrum Extrapolation for Parameterized Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13751)
*Zhangyong Liang,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MODE方法解决参数化PINNs在OOD泛化中的问题，通过轻量级微架构实现物理算子适应，优于传统SVD和PEFT方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SVD微调存在刚性子空间锁定和高频谱模式截断问题，而常规PEFT方法如LoRA在参数化PINNs中引入严重的帕累托权衡，增加参数开销并破坏物理流形结构

Method: 提出MODE方法，包含三个互补机制：主谱密集混合（在冻结正交基内实现跨模态能量转移）、残差谱唤醒（通过单个可训练标量激活高频谱分量）和仿射伽利略解锁（显式分离空间平移动力学）

Result: 在1D对流-扩散-反应方程和2D亥姆霍兹方程等挑战性PDE基准测试中，MODE实现了强大的OOD泛化能力，同时保持了原生SVD的最小参数复杂度，并优于现有PEFT基线

Conclusion: MODE为物理算子适应提供了一种轻量级微架构，有效解决了SVD和传统PEFT方法的局限性，在保持参数效率的同时显著提升了OOD泛化性能

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have achieved notable success in modeling dynamical systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs). To avoid computationally expensive retraining under new physical conditions, parameterized PINNs (P$^2$INNs) commonly adapt pre-trained operators using singular value decomposition (SVD) for out-of-distribution (OOD) regimes. However, SVD-based fine-tuning often suffers from rigid subspace locking and truncation of important high-frequency spectral modes, limiting its ability to capture complex physical transitions. While parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods appear to be promising alternatives, applying conventional adapters such as LoRA to P$^2$INNs introduces a severe Pareto trade-off, as additive updates increase parameter overhead and disrupt the structured physical manifolds inherent in operator representations. To address these limitations, we propose Manifold-Orthogonal Dual-spectrum Extrapolation (MODE), a lightweight micro-architecture designed for physics operator adaptation. MODE decomposes physical evolution into complementary mechanisms including principal-spectrum dense mixing that enables cross-modal energy transfer within frozen orthogonal bases, residual-spectrum awakening that activates high-frequency spectral components through a single trainable scalar, and affine Galilean unlocking that explicitly isolates spatial translation dynamics. Experiments on challenging PDE benchmarks including the 1D Convection--Diffusion--Reaction equation and the 2D Helmholtz equation demonstrate that MODE achieves strong out-of-distribution generalization while preserving the minimal parameter complexity of native SVD and outperforming existing PEFT-based baselines.

</details>


### [153] [Greedy Information Projection for LLM Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13790)
*Victor Ye Dong,Kuan-Yun Lee,Jiamei Shuai,Shengfei Liu,Yi Liu,Jian Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GIP是一个基于互信息最大化的训练样本选择框架，通过优化查询信号与数据子集之间的互信息，平衡质量和多样性，实现高效微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为大型语言模型微调提供一种原则性的训练样本选择方法，解决传统方法在质量和多样性平衡方面的不足，减少计算资源消耗。

Method: 将样本选择问题形式化为最大化查询信号与数据子集之间的互信息，使用闭式互信息目标函数，通过贪婪匹配追踪算法进行优化，该优化等价于最大化查询嵌入矩阵到选定数据张成空间的投影。

Result: 在指令跟随和数学推理数据集上，GIP选择的小规模子集能够达到全数据微调的性能，同时显著减少所需样本数量和计算资源。

Conclusion: GIP提供了一个统一质量和多样性感知选择的框架，通过互信息最大化实现高效微调，其几何解释揭示了质量和多样性共同涌现的原因。

Abstract: We present \emph{Greedy Information Projection} (\textsc{GIP}), a principled framework for choosing training examples for large language model fine-tuning. \textsc{GIP} casts selection as maximizing mutual information between a subset of examples and task-specific query signals, which may originate from LLM quality judgments, metadata, or other sources. The framework involves optimizing a closed-form mutual information objective defined using both data and query embeddings, naturally balancing {\it quality} and {\it diversity}. Optimizing this score is equivalent to maximizing the projection of the query embedding matrix onto the span of the selected data, which provides a geometric explanation for the co-emergence of quality and diversity. Building on this view, we employ a fast greedy matching-pursuit procedure with efficient projection-based updates. On instruction-following and mathematical reasoning datasets, \textsc{GIP} selects small subsets that match full-data fine-tuning while using only a fraction of examples and compute, unifying quality-aware and diversity-aware selection for efficient fine-tuning.

</details>


### [154] [IGU-LoRA: Adaptive Rank Allocation via Integrated Gradients and Uncertainty-Aware Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13792)
*Xuan Cui,Huiyue Li,Run Zeng,Yunfei Zhao,Jinrui Qian,Wei Duan,Bo Liu,Zhanpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IGU-LoRA：一种基于层内积分梯度和不确定性感知的自适应秩LoRA方法，通过路径敏感度估计和噪声抑制机制，在相同参数预算下优于现有PEFT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LoRA在所有层使用统一秩分配，忽略了层间重要性差异；现有自适应秩方法（如AdaLoRA）依赖瞬时梯度，仅捕捉局部敏感度，忽略了层内路径效应，导致不稳定和偏置的评分。

Method: 提出IGU-LoRA：1）计算层内积分梯度（IG）敏感度并聚合为层级评分用于秩分配；2）应用不确定性感知方案，使用指数移动平均和偏差跟踪来抑制噪声更新并校准秩选择；3）理论上证明了参数空间IG的复合梯形法则近似误差上界。

Result: 在多样化任务和架构上，IGU-LoRA在匹配参数预算下始终优于强PEFT基线，提高了下游任务准确性和鲁棒性；消融实验证实了路径敏感度估计和不确定性感知选择对有效秩分配的贡献。

Conclusion: IGU-LoRA通过结合层内路径敏感度估计和不确定性感知机制，实现了更稳定和有效的自适应秩分配，为参数高效微调提供了改进方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) scale to billions of parameters, full-parameter fine-tuning becomes compute- and memory-prohibitive. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) mitigates this issue by updating only a small set of task-specific parameters while keeping the base model frozen. Among PEFT approaches, low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is widely adopted; however, it enforces a uniform rank across layers despite substantial variation in layer importance, motivating {layerwise} rank allocation. Recent adaptive-rank variants (e.g., AdaLoRA) allocate ranks based on importance scores, yet typically rely on instantaneous gradients that capture only local sensitivity, overlooking non-local, pathwise effects within the same layer, which yields unstable and biased scores. To address this limitation, we introduce IGU-LoRA, an adaptive-rank LoRA that (i) computes within-layer Integrated Gradients (IG) sensitivities and aggregates them into a layer-level score for rank allocation, and (ii) applies an uncertainty-aware scheme using exponential moving averages with deviation tracking to suppress noisy updates and calibrate rank selection. Theoretically, we prove an upper bound on the composite trapezoidal rule approximation error for parameter-space IG under a pathwise Hessian-Lipschitz condition, which informs the quadrature budget. Across diverse tasks and architectures, IGU-LoRA consistently outperforms strong PEFT baselines at matched parameter budgets, improving downstream accuracy and robustness. Ablations confirm the contributions of pathwise within-layer sensitivity estimates and uncertainty-aware selection to effective rank allocation. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/withyou12/igulora.git

</details>


### [155] [Computation and Communication Efficient Federated Unlearning via On-server Gradient Conflict Mitigation and Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13795)
*Minh-Duong Nguyen,Senura Hansaja,Le-Tuan Nguyen,Quoc-Viet Pham,Ken-Tye Yong,Nguyen H. Tran,Dung D. Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联邦遗忘学习框架FOUL，通过两阶段方法（学习遗忘阶段和服务器端知识聚合阶段）高效移除特定参与者的数据贡献，解决联邦遗忘学习中的隐私和效率挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦遗忘学习面临跨客户端知识不可访问、高计算和通信成本等挑战，需要一种既能保护隐私又能高效移除特定参与者数据贡献的解决方案

Method: 提出FOUL框架，包含两个关键阶段：1) 学习遗忘阶段：识别并编码遗忘客户端的关键特征，建立高效通信基础；2) 服务器端知识聚合阶段：在服务器端执行遗忘过程，无需访问客户端数据

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，FOUL在联邦遗忘学习中优于重新训练方法，实现竞争性或更优的结果，同时显著减少遗忘时间，保持低通信和计算成本

Conclusion: FOUL框架有效解决了联邦遗忘学习的挑战，通过创新的两阶段方法实现了高效、隐私保护的遗忘过程，并提出了新的评估指标和更透明的数据设置

Abstract: Federated Unlearning (FUL) aims to remove specific participants' data contributions from a trained Federated Learning model, thereby ensuring data privacy and compliance with regulatory requirements. Despite its potential, progress in FUL has been limited due to several challenges, including the cross-client knowledge inaccessibility and high computational and communication costs. To overcome these challenges, we propose Federated On-server Unlearning (FOUL), a novel framework that comprises two key stages. The learning-to-unlearn stage serves as a preparatory learning phase, during which the model identifies and encodes the key features associated with the forget clients. This stage is communication-efficient and establishes the basis for the subsequent unlearning process. Subsequently, on-server knowledge aggregation phase aims to perform the unlearning process at the server without requiring access to client data, thereby preserving both efficiency and privacy. We introduce a new data setting for FUL, which enables a more transparent and rigorous evaluation of unlearning. To highlight the effectiveness of our approach, we propose a novel evaluation metric termed time-to-forget, which measures how quickly the model achieves optimal unlearning performance. Extensive experiments conducted on three datasets under various unlearning scenarios demonstrate that FOUL outperforms the Retraining in FUL. Moreover, FOUL achieves competitive or superior results with significantly reduced time-to-forget, while maintaining low communication and computation costs.

</details>


### [156] [Node Role-Guided LLMs for Dynamic Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13799)
*Dongyuan Li,Ying Zhang,Yaozu Wu,Renhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DyG-RoLLM：一个端到端可解释的动态图聚类框架，通过可学习原型将连续图嵌入映射到离散语义概念，并利用分层LLM推理机制生成聚类结果和自然语言解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图聚类方法多为黑盒模型，缺乏聚类决策的可解释性，无法提供聚类形成和演化的语义解释，限制了在医疗、交通等安全关键领域的应用。

Method: 1) 将节点表示分解为正交的角色子空间和聚类子空间；2) 在角色子空间中引入五个节点角色原型（Leader、Contributor、Wanderer、Connector、Newcomer）作为语义锚点；3) 设计分层LLM推理机制生成聚类结果和自然语言解释，同时提供一致性反馈作为弱监督来优化节点表示。

Result: 在四个合成数据集和六个真实世界基准测试上的实验结果表明，DyG-RoLLM在有效性、可解释性和鲁棒性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: DyG-RoLLM通过将连续嵌入映射到离散语义概念并利用LLM推理，实现了动态图聚类的可解释性，为安全关键领域提供了可靠的分析工具。

Abstract: Dynamic graph clustering aims to detect and track time-varying clusters in dynamic graphs, revealing how complex real-world systems evolve over time. However, existing methods are predominantly black-box models. They lack interpretability in their clustering decisions and fail to provide semantic explanations of why clusters form or how they evolve, severely limiting their use in safety-critical domains such as healthcare or transportation. To address these limitations, we propose an end-to-end interpretable framework that maps continuous graph embeddings into discrete semantic concepts through learnable prototypes. Specifically, we first decompose node representations into orthogonal role and clustering subspaces, so that nodes with similar roles (e.g., hubs, bridges) but different cluster affiliations can be properly distinguished. We then introduce five node role prototypes (Leader, Contributor, Wanderer, Connector, Newcomer) in the role subspace as semantic anchors, transforming continuous embeddings into discrete concepts to facilitate LLM understanding of node roles within communities. Finally, we design a hierarchical LLM reasoning mechanism to generate both clustering results and natural language explanations, while providing consistency feedback as weak supervision to refine node representations. Experimental results on four synthetic and six real-world benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness, interpretability, and robustness of DyG-RoLLM. Code is available at https://github.com/Clearloveyuan/DyG-RoLLM.

</details>


### [157] [Prototypical Exemplar Condensation for Memory-efficient Online Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13804)
*Minh-Duong Nguyen,Thien-Thanh Dao,Le-Tuan Nguyen,Dung D. Le,Kok-Seng Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于原型样本合成的持续学习方法，通过存储少量代表性原型样本来压缩内存占用，同时引入扰动增强机制提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于复现的持续学习方法需要存储大量样本（每类超过20个）来维持性能，内存占用大。本文旨在进一步压缩内存足迹，同时保护隐私

Method: 1) 合成并存储原型样本，这些样本通过特征提取器后能形成代表性原型；2) 引入基于扰动的增强机制，在训练时生成先前数据的合成变体

Result: 在广泛使用的基准数据集和设置上的评估表明，该算法相比现有基线实现了更优性能，特别是在大规模数据集和任务数量多的场景中

Conclusion: 通过原型样本合成和扰动增强，能够在仅使用少量样本的情况下有效保留先前知识，显著压缩内存占用并提升持续学习性能

Abstract: Rehearsal-based continual learning (CL) mitigates catastrophic forgetting by maintaining a subset of samples from previous tasks for replay. Existing studies primarily focus on optimizing memory storage through coreset selection strategies. While these methods are effective, they typically require storing a substantial number of samples per class (SPC), often exceeding 20, to maintain satisfactory performance. In this work, we propose to further compress the memory footprint by synthesizing and storing prototypical exemplars, which can form representative prototypes when passed through the feature extractor. Owing to their representative nature, these exemplars enable the model to retain previous knowledge using only a small number of samples while preserving privacy. Moreover, we introduce a perturbation-based augmentation mechanism that generates synthetic variants of previous data during training, thereby enhancing CL performance. Extensive evaluations on widely used benchmark datasets and settings demonstrate that the proposed algorithm achieves superior performance compared to existing baselines, particularly in scenarios involving large-scale datasets and a high number of tasks.

</details>


### [158] [Effective Sparsity: A Unified Framework via Normalized Entropy and the Effective Number of Nonzeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13826)
*Haoyu He,Hao Wang,Jiashan Wang,Hao Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的有效稀疏性度量方法ENZ，替代传统的l0范数，通过熵正则化量化显著系数的集中程度，在噪声线性逆问题中提供更稳定和连续的稀疏性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏促进方法依赖l0范数，将所有非零分量视为同等重要。但在实际逆问题中，解通常包含许多对重建影响很小的小幅值分量，这会导致对信号复杂性的高估。需要一种能区分显著分量和可忽略分量的稀疏性度量方法。

Method: 提出有效非零数(ENZ)方法，这是一类基于归一化熵的正则化器，包括Shannon和Renyi形式。ENZ量化显著系数的集中程度，提供稳定且连续的稀疏性度量。在限制等距性质(RIP)框架下建立了理论保证，证明ENZ恢复的唯一性和稳定性。

Result: ENZ将有效稀疏性分解为支撑基数乘以分布效率项，从而将熵与l0正则化联系起来。数值实验表明，该有效稀疏性框架在鲁棒性和准确性方面优于传统的基于基数的方法。

Conclusion: ENZ提供了一种比传统l0范数更优越的有效稀疏性度量方法，能够更好地处理实际逆问题中存在的可忽略小幅值分量，提高了稀疏恢复的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Classical sparsity promoting methods rely on the l0 norm, which treats all nonzero components as equally significant. In practical inverse problems, however, solutions often exhibit many small amplitude components that have little effect on reconstruction but lead to an overestimation of signal complexity. We address this limitation by shifting the paradigm from discrete cardinality to effective sparsity. Our approach introduces the effective number of nonzeros (ENZ), a unified class of normalized entropy-based regularizers, including Shannon and Renyi forms, that quantifies the concentration of significant coefficients. We show that, unlike the classical l0 norm, the ENZ provides a stable and continuous measure of effective sparsity that is insensitive to negligible perturbations. For noisy linear inverse problems, we establish theoretical guarantees under the Restricted Isometry Property (RIP), proving that ENZ based recovery is unique and stable. We also derive a decomposition showing that the ENZ equals the support cardinality times a distributional efficiency term, thereby linking entropy with l0 regularization. Numerical experiments show that this effective sparsity framework outperforms traditional cardinality based methods in robustness and accuracy.

</details>


### [159] [Exploring the Dimensions of a Variational Neuron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13849)
*Yves Ruffenach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVE是一种变分分布神经元，将概率结构定位到神经元层面，包含显式先验、摊销后验和单元级变分正则化，通过改变内部潜在维度k来研究神经元的学习机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代架构中不确定性主要通过全局潜在变量或参数不确定性建模，而计算单元本身保持标量。EVE旨在将概率结构重新定位到神经元层面，使其在局部可观察和可控制。

Method: 提出EVE作为局部概率计算单元，研究内部潜在维度k（从k=1到高维）如何影响神经元的学习机制，并探讨与局部容量控制和通过神经元级自回归扩展实现的时间持久性这两个结构特性的相互作用。

Result: 通过内部诊断和约束（包括有效KL、mu^2的目标带、带外分数、漂移和崩溃指标）在预测和表格设置中展示，潜在维度、控制和时态扩展塑造神经元的内部机制，某些神经元级变量是可测量、信息丰富且与下游行为相关的。

Conclusion: 本文通过实验为基础，首次绘制了变分神经元开启的设计空间地图，展示了神经元级概率计算单元的可行性和研究价值。

Abstract: We introduce EVE (Elemental Variational Expanse), a variational distributional neuron formulated as a local probabilistic computational unit with an explicit prior, an amortized posterior, and unit-level variational regularization. In most modern architectures, uncertainty is modeled through global latent variables or parameter uncertainty, while the computational unit itself remains scalar. EVE instead relocates probabilistic structure to the neuron level, making it locally observable and controllable.
  In this paper, the term dimensions refers primarily to the neuron's internal latent dimensionality, denoted by k. We study how varying k, from the atomic case k = 1 to higher-dimensional latent spaces, changes the neuron's learned operating regime. We then examine how this main axis interacts with two additional structural properties: local capacity control and temporal persistence through a neuron-level autoregressive extension.
  To support this study, EVE is instrumented with internal diagnostics and constraints, including effective KL, a target band on mu^2, out-of-band fractions, and indicators of drift and collapse. Across selected forecasting and tabular settings, we show that latent dimensionality, control, and temporal extension shape the neuron's internal regime, and that some neuron-level variables are measurable, informative, and related to downstream behavior. Overall, the paper provides an experimentally grounded first map of the design space opened by a variational neuron.

</details>


### [160] [OrigamiBench: An Interactive Environment to Synthesize Flat-Foldable Origamis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13856)
*Naaisha Agarwal,Yihan Wu,Yichang Jian,Yikuan Hu,Nishad Mansoor,Mohan Li,Yifei Peng,Wang-Zhou Dai,Yao-Xiang Ding,Emanuele Sansone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文介绍了OrigamiBench基准测试，用于评估AI系统在折纸任务中的物理因果推理能力，发现仅扩大模型规模无法可靠产生物理变换的因果推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能够在物理世界中规划、行动和创造的AI系统需要超越模式识别，理解支配物理过程的因果机制和约束。现有基准通常将视觉感知和程序推理视为独立问题，缺乏整合两者的测试平台。

Method: 引入OrigamiBench交互式基准测试，模型迭代提出折叠操作并接收物理有效性和目标配置相似性的反馈。折纸领域自然整合了视觉感知、几何物理约束推理和序列规划。

Result: 现代视觉语言模型的实验表明，仅扩大模型规模无法可靠产生物理变换的因果推理。模型无法生成连贯的多步折叠策略，表明视觉和语言表示仍然弱整合。

Conclusion: 折纸为评估AI系统的物理因果推理能力提供了结构化测试平台，当前模型在整合视觉和语言表示以进行序列物理推理方面存在不足，需要新的方法超越单纯规模扩展。

Abstract: Building AI systems that can plan, act, and create in the physical world requires more than pattern recognition. Such systems must understand the causal mechanisms and constraints governing physical processes in order to guide sequential decisions. This capability relies on internal representations, analogous to an internal language model, that relate observations, actions, and resulting environmental changes. However, many existing benchmarks treat visual perception and programmatic reasoning as separate problems, focusing either on visual recognition or on symbolic tasks. The domain of origami provides a natural testbed that integrates these modalities. Constructing shapes through folding operations requires visual perception, reasoning about geometric and physical constraints, and sequential planning, while remaining sufficiently structured for systematic evaluation. We introduce OrigamiBench, an interactive benchmark in which models iteratively propose folds and receive feedback on physical validity and similarity to a target configuration. Experiments with modern vision-language models show that scaling model size alone does not reliably produce causal reasoning about physical transformations. Models fail to generate coherent multi-step folding strategies, suggesting that visual and language representations remain weakly integrated.

</details>


### [161] [UVLM: A Universal Vision-Language Model Loader for Reproducible Multimodal Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13893)
*Joan Perez,Giovanni Fusco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UVLM是一个统一的视觉语言模型加载框架，支持LLaVA-NeXT和Qwen2.5-VL等异构架构，提供标准化接口进行配置、推理和基准测试，特别适用于街道图像分析任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型存在显著的架构异构性，这阻碍了实际部署和跨模型比较。不同模型家族在视觉编码、分词和解码策略上存在根本差异，使得研究人员难以使用统一的接口和评估协议进行公平比较。

Method: 提出UVLM框架，通过单一推理函数抽象不同VLM架构的差异，支持多任务提示构建器（数值、类别、布尔、文本四种响应类型）、基于多数投票的共识验证机制、灵活令牌预算（最高1500个令牌）以及内置思维链参考模式。框架在Google Colab上部署，使用消费级GPU资源。

Result: 成功实现了对LLaVA-NeXT和Qwen2.5-VL两大模型家族的统一支持，并在包含120张街景图像的语料库上首次对不同VLM进行了递增推理复杂度的基准测试，展示了框架的实用性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: UVLM为异构视觉语言模型提供了可复现、易访问且可扩展的统一接口，解决了实际部署中的架构差异问题，使研究人员能够公平比较不同模型在自定义图像分析任务上的性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for image understanding tasks, yet their practical deployment remains hindered by significant architectural heterogeneity across model families. This paper introduces UVLM (Universal Vision-Language Model Loader), a Google Colab-based framework that provides a unified interface for loading, configuring, and benchmarking multiple VLM architectures on custom image analysis tasks. UVLM currently supports two major model families -- LLaVA-NeXT and Qwen2.5-VL -- which differ fundamentally in their vision encoding, tokenization, and decoding strategies. The framework abstracts these differences behind a single inference function, enabling researchers to compare models using identical prompts and evaluation protocols. Key features include a multi-task prompt builder with support for four response types (numeric, category, boolean, text), a consensus validation mechanism based on majority voting across repeated inferences, a flexible token budget (up to 1,500 tokens) enabling users to design custom reasoning strategies through prompt engineering, and a built-in chain-of-thought reference mode for benchmarking. UVLM is designed for reproducibility, accessibility, and extensibility and as such is freely deployable on Google Colab using consumer-grade GPU resources. The paper also presents the first benchmarking of different VLMs on tasks of increasing reasoning complexity using a corpus of 120 street-view images.

</details>


### [162] [Robust Self-Training with Closed-loop Label Correction for Learning from Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13894)
*Zhanhui Lin,Yanlin Liu,Sanping Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于解耦双层优化的自训练标签校正框架，通过分类器和神经校正函数的协同进化来处理带噪声标签的深度学习问题，在减少训练时间的同时达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理标签噪声的方法（如转移矩阵、噪声检测、元学习）通常存在噪声样本利用率低和计算成本高的问题，需要一种更高效实用的解决方案。

Method: 采用解耦双层优化框架，让分类器和神经校正函数协同进化；利用小规模干净数据集，通过噪声后验模拟和中间特征传递真实知识，形成防止误差放大的闭环反馈系统。

Result: 在CIFAR和Clothing1M等基准数据集上实现了最先进的性能，同时显著减少了训练时间，证明了方法的实用性和高效性。

Conclusion: 提出的自训练标签校正框架通过解耦双层优化和闭环反馈系统，有效解决了带噪声标签的深度学习问题，在理论和实验上都验证了其稳定性和优越性。

Abstract: Training deep neural networks with noisy labels remains a significant challenge, often leading to degraded performance. Existing methods for handling label noise typically rely on either transition matrix, noise detection, or meta-learning techniques, but they often exhibit low utilization efficiency of noisy samples and incur high computational costs. In this paper, we propose a self-training label correction framework using decoupled bilevel optimization, where a classifier and neural correction function co-evolve. Leveraging a small clean dataset, our method employs noisy posterior simulation and intermediate features to transfer ground-truth knowledge, forming a closed-loop feedback system that prevents error amplification. Theoretical guarantees underpin the stability of our approach, and extensive experiments on benchmark datasets like CIFAR and Clothing1M confirm state-of-the-art performance with reduced training time, highlighting its practical applicability for learning from noisy labels.

</details>


### [163] [FedPBS: Proximal-Balanced Scaling Federated Learning Model for Robust Personalized Training for Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13909)
*Eman M. AbouNassara,Amr Elshalla,Sameh Abdulah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedPBS：一种结合FedBS和FedProx思想的联邦学习算法，通过动态调整批量大小和选择性应用近端校正来解决统计异构性和客户端参与不均问题，在非IID数据下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在保护本地数据隐私的同时面临统计异构性和客户端参与不均的挑战，这些因素会降低收敛速度和模型质量。现有方法如FedBS和FedProx各有优势但未能完全解决这些问题。

Method: FedPBS结合了FedBS和FedProx的互补思想：1）动态调整批量大小以适应客户端资源，支持平衡且可扩展的参与；2）对小批量客户端选择性应用近端校正，稳定本地更新并减少与全局模型的偏差。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和UCI-HAR等基准数据集的高度非IID设置下，FedPBS持续优于FedBS、FedGA、MOON和FedProx等最先进方法。在极端数据异构性下表现出稳健的性能提升，平滑的损失曲线表明在不同联邦环境中具有稳定的收敛性。

Conclusion: FedPBS通过动态批量大小调整和选择性近端校正，有效解决了联邦学习中的统计异构性和客户端参与不均问题，在非IID条件下实现了优于现有方法的性能和稳定收敛。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables a set of distributed clients to jointly train machine learning models while preserving their local data privacy, making it attractive for applications in healthcare, finance, mobility, and smart-city systems. However, FL faces several challenges, including statistical heterogeneity and uneven client participation, which can degrade convergence and model quality. In this work, we propose FedPBS, an FL algorithm that couples complementary ideas from FedBS and FedProx to address these challenges. FedPBS dynamically adapts batch sizes to client resources to support balanced and scalable participation, and selectively applies a proximal correction to small-batch clients to stabilize local updates and reduce divergence from the global model. Experiments on benchmarking datasets such as CIFAR-10 and UCI-HAR under highly non-IID settings demonstrate that FedPBS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, including FedBS, FedGA, MOON, and FedProx. The results demonstrate robust performance gains under extreme data heterogeneity, with smooth loss curves indicating stable convergence across diverse federated environments. FedPBS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art federated learning baselines on UCI-HAR and CIFAR-10 under severe non-IID conditions while maintaining stable and reliable convergence.

</details>


### [164] [Close to Reality: Interpretable and Feasible Data Augmentation for Imbalanced Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13927)
*Matheus Camilo da Silva,Gabriel Gustavo Costanzo,Andrea de Lorenzo,Sylvio Barbon Junior*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPG-da是一个用于不平衡数据集过采样的可解释框架，通过从训练模型中提取决策谓词来确保生成的样本满足领域规则，提高分类性能的同时保证逻辑有效性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统过采样技术存在两个主要问题：1）容易生成不现实或不可行的样本；2）缺乏可解释性，难以追踪效果和进行调整。这些黑盒方法可能无法带来显著的性能提升。

Method: 提出DPG-da框架，从训练模型中提取可解释的决策谓词来捕获领域规则，并在样本生成过程中强制执行这些规则。该方法确保过采样数据保持多样性、满足约束条件且可解释。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准数据集上的实验表明，DPG-da在分类性能上持续优于传统过采样方法，同时保证逻辑有效性，并提供清晰可解释的过采样数据说明。

Conclusion: DPG-da成功解决了传统过采样方法的局限性，通过可解释的决策谓词框架实现了更有效、更可靠的样本生成，为不平衡数据集分类提供了透明且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Many machine learning classification tasks involve imbalanced datasets, which are often subject to over-sampling techniques aimed at improving model performance. However, these techniques are prone to generating unrealistic or infeasible samples. Furthermore, they often function as black boxes, lacking interpretability in their procedures. This opacity makes it difficult to track their effectiveness and provide necessary adjustments, and they may ultimately fail to yield significant performance improvements.
  To bridge this gap, we introduce the Decision Predicate Graphs for Data Augmentation (DPG-da), a framework that extracts interpretable decision predicates from trained models to capture domain rules and enforce them during sample generation. This design ensures that over-sampled data remain diverse, constraint-satisfying, and interpretable. In experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmark datasets, DPG-da consistently improves classification performance over traditional over-sampling methods, while guaranteeing logical validity and offering clear, interpretable explanations of the over-sampled data.

</details>


### [165] [Shapes are not enough: CONSERVAttack and its use for finding vulnerabilities and uncertainties in machine learning applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13970)
*Philip Bechtle,Lucie Flek,Philipp Alexander Jung,Akbar Karimi,Timo Saala,Alexander Schmidt,Matthias Schott,Philipp Soldin,Christopher Wiebusch,Ulrich Willemsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CONSERVAttack对抗攻击方法，利用粒子物理中模拟与数据间未检测到的偏差空间，在不确定性范围内生成对抗扰动以欺骗深度学习模型，同时提出缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前粒子物理中深度学习模型验证主要依赖物理动机驱动的系统性不确定性测试和特定控制区域的分布比较，但这些方法无法保证检测所有可能的模拟与数据偏差。需要探索剩余偏差空间对模型的影响。

Method: 提出CONSERVAttack对抗攻击方法，该方法在现有不确定性测试框架内生成对抗扰动，确保扰动符合不确定性边界，能够逃避标准验证检查，同时成功欺骗底层深度学习模型。

Result: CONSERVAttack能够生成在不确定性范围内一致的对抗扰动，这些扰动能够逃避标准验证检查，同时成功欺骗粒子物理中的深度学习模型，揭示了现有验证方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 粒子物理中深度学习的鲁棒性必须考虑对抗效应，需要开发更全面的验证方法以确保模型可靠性，CONSERVAttack揭示了现有验证框架的漏洞并提供了改进方向。

Abstract: In High Energy Physics, as in many other fields of science, the application of machine learning techniques has been crucial in advancing our understanding of fundamental phenomena. Increasingly, deep learning models are applied to analyze both simulated and experimental data. In most experiments, a rigorous regime of testing for physically motivated systematic uncertainties is in place. The numerical evaluation of these tests for differences between the data on the one side and simulations on the other side quantifies the effect of potential sources of mismodelling on the machine learning output. In addition, thorough comparisons of marginal distributions and (linear) feature correlations between data and simulation in "control regions" are applied. However, the guidance by physical motivation, and the need to constrain comparisons to specific regions, does not guarantee that all possible sources of deviations have been accounted for. We therefore propose a new adversarial attack - the CONSERVAttack - designed to exploit the remaining space of hypothetical deviations between simulation and data after the above mentioned tests. The resulting adversarial perturbations are consistent within the uncertainty bounds - evading standard validation checks - while successfully fooling the underlying model. We further propose strategies to mitigate such vulnerabilities and argue that robustness to adversarial effects must be considered when interpreting results from deep learning in particle physics.

</details>


### [166] [Chunk-Guided Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13971)
*Gwanwoo Song,Kwanyoung Park,Youngwoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CGQ是一种离线强化学习算法，通过将单步TD critic正则化向基于动作块的critic，减少长期视野下的自举误差累积，同时保持细粒度的价值传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中，单步时序差分学习在长期视野下会遭受自举误差累积问题，而基于动作块的方法虽然能缓解这一问题，但会限制策略类为开环动作序列，可能引入次优性。

Method: 提出Chunk-Guided Q-Learning (CGQ)，这是一种单步TD算法，通过将细粒度单步critic正则化向使用时间扩展备份训练的基于动作块的critic，来指导单步critic的学习。

Result: 理论上证明CGQ获得了比单独使用单步或动作块TD学习更紧的critic最优性边界。在具有挑战性的长视野OGBench任务上，CGQ实现了强大性能，通常优于单步和动作块方法。

Conclusion: CGQ通过结合单步和动作块TD学习的优势，解决了离线强化学习中长期视野下的误差累积问题，同时保持了细粒度的策略表达能力。

Abstract: In offline reinforcement learning (RL), single-step temporal-difference (TD) learning can suffer from bootstrapping error accumulation over long horizons. Action-chunked TD methods mitigate this by backing up over multiple steps, but can introduce suboptimality by restricting the policy class to open-loop action sequences. To resolve this trade-off, we present Chunk-Guided Q-Learning (CGQ), a single-step TD algorithm that guides a fine-grained single-step critic by regularizing it toward a chunk-based critic trained using temporally extended backups. This reduces compounding error while preserving fine-grained value propagation. We theoretically show that CGQ attains tighter critic optimality bounds than either single-step or action-chunked TD learning alone. Empirically, CGQ achieves strong performance on challenging long-horizon OGBench tasks, often outperforming both single-step and action-chunked methods.

</details>


### [167] [Aumann-SHAP: The Geometry of Counterfactual Interaction Explanations in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14014)
*Adam Belahcen,Stéphane Mussard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Aumann-SHAP是一个交互感知框架，通过将模型限制在连接基线和反事实特征的局部超立方体中，分解反事实转换。它将超立方体分解为网格，构建微观玩家合作博弈，使用Shapley和LES值分析特征交互贡献和反事实分析贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法通常缺乏对特征交互作用的深入分析，无法量化特征间相互作用对反事实转换的贡献。需要一种能够同时提供交互解释性、个体解释性和全局解释性的框架。

Method: 提出Aumann-SHAP框架：1) 将反事实转换限制在基线和反事实特征构成的局部超立方体中；2) 将超立方体分解为网格，构建微观玩家合作博弈，其中基本网格步移动作为玩家；3) 在该TU微观博弈上计算Shapley值和LES值，分析特征交互贡献和反事实分析贡献；4) 证明这些值收敛到对角线Aumann-Shapley（集成梯度）归因方法。

Result: 在德国信用数据集和MNIST数据上的实验表明，Aumann-LES在反事实转换过程中产生稳健结果，比标准Shapley值提供更好的解释。该方法能够：1) 量化每个特征与其他特征的交互贡献；2) 量化每个实例和每个特征对反事实分析的贡献。

Conclusion: Aumann-SHAP框架成功地将交互感知纳入反事实解释，通过微观博弈方法同时提供交互解释性、个体解释性和全局解释性。Aumann-LES值在反事实转换中表现优于标准Shapley值，为特征交互分析提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We introduce Aumann-SHAP, an interaction-aware framework that decomposes counterfactual transitions by restricting the model to a local hypercube connecting baseline and counterfactual features. Each hyper-cube is decomposed into a grid in order to construct an induced micro-player cooperative game in which elementary grid-step moves become players. Shapley and LES values on this TU-micro-game yield: (i) within-pot contribution of each feature to the interaction with other features (interaction explainability), and (ii) the contribution of each instance and each feature to the counterfactual analysis (individual and global explainability). In particular, Aumann-LES values produce individual and global explanations along the counterfactual transition. Shapley and LES values converge to the diagonal Aumann-Shapley (integrated-gradients) attribution method. Experiments on the German Credit dataset and MNIST data show that Aumann-LES produces robust results and better explanations than the standard Shapley value during the counterfactual transition.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Beyond First-Order Methods for $\ell_p$-Structured Non-Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13491)
*Abhijeet Vyas,Brian Bullins*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了针对ℓp结构化非单调变分不等式的高阶算法，解决了弱Minty变分不等式(weak-MVI)参数ρ>0时ℓp范数稳定点的收敛问题，填补了先前工作仅限于ρ=0的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diakonikolas等人(2021)的工作虽然引入了弱MVI设置，但只能找到ρ=0时的ℓp范数稳定点，这限制了在ℓp范数设置下的应用。本文旨在解决这一局限性，为ρ>0的情况提供收敛保证。

Method: 提出了一套高阶方法，能够在合适的ρ>0范围内收敛到ℓp范数稳定点。方法包括：1)针对ℓp结构化非单调变分不等式的高阶算法；2)将Adil等人(2022)的高阶光滑单调算子收敛结果推广到p≥2的情况；3)将欧几里得技术扩展到连续时间设置。

Result: 成功实现了在ρ>0时ℓp范数稳定点的收敛，突破了先前工作仅限于ρ=0的限制。同时扩展了高阶光滑单调算子的收敛范围，并将方法推广到连续时间设置。

Conclusion: 本文填补了ℓp范数设置下弱MVI参数ρ>0时的理论空白，提供了一套有效的高阶算法，为ℓp结构化非单调变分不等式问题提供了更广泛的收敛保证。

Abstract: We propose novel high-order algorithms for a class of $\ell_p$-structured non-monotone variational inequalities. In particular, work by Diakonikolas et al. (2021), which introduced the weak Minty variational inequality (weak-MVI) setting, showed how to find an approximate first-order Euclidean stationary point for a strictly positive range of the weak-MVI parameter $ρ$. However, for the $\ell_p$-norm stationary point setting ($p \neq 2$), their guarantees are limited to $ρ=0$, which recovers the standard MVI setting. In this work, we address this gap by presenting a suite of high-order methods that converge to $\ell_p$-norm stationary points for a suitable range of $ρ> 0$, thereby circumventing previous fundamental challenges in $\ell_p$ settings. We further show convergence for high-order smooth \textit{monotone} operators, generalizing Adil et al. (2022) to the case where $p \geq 2$, and we extend our Euclidean techniques to continuous-time settings.

</details>


### [169] [Hybrid topology control: a dynamic leader-based distributed edge-addition and deletion mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13529)
*Kunal Garg,Xi Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于领导者的多机器人系统连接维护算法，通过中心节点进行实时重配置，减少决策时间并处理未知动态扰动和通信延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统需要保持通信连接来完成团队目标，但连接维护限制了机器人移动性，导致团队性能下降。现有基于共识的连接维护方法决策时间随节点数量增加，需要多轮通信，在通信延迟下效果不佳。

Method: 提出基于领导者的决策算法，使用中心节点进行高效实时重配置；开发了考虑未知扰动和通信延迟的机器人位置估计方法；中心节点根据位置估计选择要删除的边，允许形成新边，保持新图的直径在阈值内。

Result: 算法将决策时间从依赖节点数量减少到依赖图直径，仅需一轮信息传输；通过仿真验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于领导者的连接维护算法能够有效处理未知动态扰动和通信延迟，显著减少决策时间，提高多机器人系统在现实条件下的性能。

Abstract: Coordinated operations of multi-robot systems (MRS) require agents to maintain communication connections to accomplish team objectives. However, maintaining the connections imposes costs in terms of restricted robot mobility, resulting in suboptimal team performance. In this work, we consider a realistic MRS framework in which agents are subject to unknown dynamical disturbances and experience communication delays. Most existing works on connectivity maintenance use consensus-based frameworks for graph reconfiguration, where decision-making time scales with the number of nodes and requires multiple rounds of communication, making them ineffective under communication delays. To address this, we propose a novel leader-based decision-making algorithm that uses a central node for efficient real-time reconfiguration, reducing decision-making time to depend on the graph diameter rather than the number of nodes and requiring only one round of information transfer through the network. We propose a novel method for estimating robot locations within the MRS that actively accounts for unknown disturbances and the communication delays. Using these position estimates, the central node selects a set of edges to delete while allowing the formation of new edges, aiming to keep the diameter of the new graph within a threshold. We provide numerous simulation results to showcase the efficacy of the proposed method.

</details>


### [170] [Single-Event Multinomial Full Kelly via Implicit State Positions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13581)
*Christopher D. Long*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于状态价格推导的凯利准则优化问题简化证明方法，通过现金头寸作为隐含头寸的概念，得到了更紧凑的公式和贪心算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的凯利准则优化问题通常使用拉格朗日乘数法进行支撑集选择，推导过程较为复杂。本文旨在提供一种更简洁、直观的状态价格推导方法，使解决方案更容易理解和记忆。

Method: 采用状态价格框架，将现金头寸c视为在每个结果上的隐含头寸（相当于在每个结果i上投入cq_i的基准赌注）。在活跃支撑集上，显式赌注只需将有利结果从基准cq_i补充到最优总赌注p_i。由此推导出公式x_i = (p_i - c q_i)_+、阈值规则p_i/q_i > c，并通过按p_i/q_i排序结果，设计单次遍历的贪心算法进行支撑集选择。

Result: 得到了与经典结果一致的凯利准则优化公式：x_i = (p_i - c q_i)_+，其中p_i为结果i的概率，q_i为状态价格，c为现金比例。阈值规则为p_i/q_i > c时结果i进入活跃支撑集。通过按p_i/q_i降序排序，可以一次性贪心地确定活跃支撑集。

Conclusion: 虽然结果在本质上与经典凯利准则相同，但隐含头寸视角提供了更紧凑的证明和更方便记忆的解决方案。该方法简化了支撑集选择的推导过程，使凯利准则优化问题的理解更加直观。

Abstract: For a single event with finitely many mutually exclusive outcomes, the full Kelly problem is to maximize expected log wealth over nonnegative stakes together with an optional cash position. The optimal formula is classical, but the support-selection step is often presented via Lagrange multipliers. This note gives a shorter state-price derivation. A cash fraction $c$ acts as an implicit position in every outcome: in terminal-wealth terms, it is equivalent to a baseline stake $cq_i$ on outcome $i$, where $q_i$ is the state price. On any active support, explicit bets therefore only top up favorable outcomes from this baseline $cq_i$ to the optimal total stake $p_i$. This yields the formula $x_i = (p_i - c q_i)_+$, the threshold rule $p_i/q_i > c$, and, after sorting outcomes by $p_i/q_i$, a one-pass greedy algorithm for support selection. The result is standard in substance, but the implicit-position viewpoint gives a compact proof and a convenient way to remember the solution.

</details>


### [171] [Feedback Control and Local Convexification of Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13588)
*Dante Kalise,Lucas M. Moschen,Grigorios A. Pavliotis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有熵项的自由能泛函的Wasserstein梯度流，通过谱分析和反馈控制实现局部指数稳定


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有熵项的自由能泛函的Wasserstein梯度流（平均场Langevin动力学）在平稳态附近的稳定性控制问题，旨在设计反馈控制律实现局部指数收敛

Method: 1. 将Wasserstein Hessian识别为势变量Hilbert空间上的自伴算子；2. 通过谱分析设计有限秩反馈控制律；3. 求解代数Riccati方程来移动闭环Hessian谱；4. 在平坦环面等流形上验证框架

Result: 1. 建立了Wasserstein Hessian与自伴算子的对应关系；2. 设计的反馈控制能将闭环Hessian谱移动到任意指定阈值δ>0以上；3. 非线性闭环流局部指数收敛到平稳态，收敛速率为δ；4. 在适当条件下，闭环能量在坐标图中局部强凸

Conclusion: 该框架为具有熵项的自由能泛函的Wasserstein梯度流提供了系统的稳定性分析和控制设计方法，可扩展到多物种系统、矩约束Fokker-Planck方程和闭黎曼流形等更一般情形

Abstract: For free energies of the form \[ F(μ) = E(μ) + σ\int_Ωμ\logμ\,dx, \quad σ> 0, \] we study the Wasserstein gradient flow, a continuity equation also known as mean-field Langevin dynamics, around a stationary state $\barμ$ on the flat torus. Our first result identifies the Wasserstein Hessian of $F$ at $\barμ$ with a self-adjoint operator with compact resolvent on a Hilbert space of potential variables, and shows that, up to the natural Riesz isometry, this operator generates the linearized gradient flow. This spectral description allows us to design a finite-rank feedback law, via an algebraic Riccati equation, that shifts the closed-loop Hessian spectrum above any prescribed threshold $δ> 0$. As a consequence, the nonlinear closed-loop flow converges locally exponentially to $\barμ$ with rate $δ$. Under an additional second-order remainder assumption on the first variation, the corresponding closed-loop energy is also locally strongly convex in chart coordinates. We illustrate the framework on the flat torus and discuss extensions to multi-species systems, moment-constrained Fokker-Planck equations, and closed Riemannian manifolds.

</details>


### [172] [The Effective Lasserre's Perturbative Positivstellensatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13954)
*Igor Klep,Victor Magron,Matthias Schötz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了无界域上多项式优化的SOS证书，通过引入加权多项式尾部扰动，给出了实现ε精度证书所需截断阶数N的显式定量界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无界域上多项式优化的SOS（平方和）证书问题。传统SOS方法在无界域上存在局限性，需要发展新的证书构造方法来处理非紧致区域上的多项式非负性验证。

Method: 基于Lasserre的扰动方法，考虑多项式p的SOS表示，并引入加权多项式尾部扰动项∑_{n=0}^N (x·x)^n/(n!)^t（0<t<1）。利用Mehler核的正性性质和多项式核方法技术，分析截断阶数N与精度ε的关系。

Result: 获得了实现ε精度证书所需截断阶数N的显式定量界限：N = O((‖p‖/ε)^{1/(1-t)})，表明N以1/ε的多项式速率增长。

Conclusion: 该方法为无界域上的多项式优化提供了有效的SOS证书构造框架，通过加权尾部扰动实现了多项式增长的计算复杂度，改进了传统方法在无界域上的局限性。

Abstract: We study sum-of-squares (SOS) certificates for nonnegative polynomials $p$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$ and their implications for polynomial optimization over unbounded domains. Building on Lasserre's perturbation approach, we consider SOS representations of $p$ augmented by weighted polynomial tails of the form $\sum_{n=0}^N (x\cdot x)^n/(n!)^t$ for $0 < t < 1$. Our main result provides an explicit quantitative bound on the truncation order $N$ required to achieve an $\varepsilon$-accurate certificate. Using positivity properties of the Mehler kernel and techniques inspired by polynomial kernel methods, we show that $N$ grows polynomially in $1/\varepsilon$, with rate $N = O((\|p\|/\varepsilon)^{1/(1-t)})$.

</details>


### [173] [Distributional Uncertainty and Adaptive Decision-Making in System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14047)
*Yujun Huang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种分布式的协同设计框架，将不确定性建模为设计问题上的分布，支持概率风险和多阶段自适应决策，超越了传统的区间边界方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定协同设计模型依赖区间边界，仅支持最坏情况推理，无法表示概率风险或多阶段自适应决策，限制了复杂工程系统的设计优化能力。

Method: 开发了协同设计的分布扩展，将不确定设计结果建模为设计问题上的分布，通过马尔可夫核重参数化支持自适应决策过程，利用拟可测和拟通用空间保持组合性。

Result: 标准协同设计互连操作在更丰富的不确定性概念下保持组合性，引入查询和观察机制提取概率设计权衡，包括可行性概率、置信边界和最小所需资源分布。

Conclusion: 分布式协同设计框架能够捕捉基于风险的敏感性和信息依赖的设计选择，这些是区间模型无法表达的，通过无人机案例研究验证了框架的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Complex engineered systems require coordinated design choices across heterogeneous components under multiple conflicting objectives and uncertain specifications. Monotone co-design provides a compositional framework for such problems by modeling each subsystem as a design problem: a feasible relation between provided functionalities and required resources in partially ordered sets. Existing uncertain co-design models rely on interval bounds, which support worst-case reasoning but cannot represent probabilistic risk or multi-stage adaptive decisions. We develop a distributional extension of co-design that models uncertain design outcomes as distributions over design problems and supports adaptive decision processes through Markov-kernel re-parameterizations. Using quasi-measurable and quasi-universal spaces, we show that the standard co-design interconnection operations remain compositional under this richer notion of uncertainty. We further introduce queries and observations that extract probabilistic design trade-offs, including feasibility probabilities, confidence bounds, and distributions of minimal required resources. A task-driven unmanned aerial vehicle case study illustrates how the framework captures risk-sensitive and information-dependent design choices that interval-based models cannot express.

</details>


### [174] [Schrödinger Bridge Over A Compact Connected Lie Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14049)
*Hamza Mahmood,Abhishek Halder,Adeel Akhtar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了紧致连通李群上运动学方程的薛定谔桥问题，旨在以最小控制代价将受控扩散过程从初始密度引导至终端密度，并提出了尊重李群几何结构的坐标无关公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究李群上薛定谔桥问题的动机在于开发一种能够避免局部参数化或欧几里得空间嵌入限制的几何方法，从而在保持李群内在结构的同时，实现概率密度之间的最优插值。

Method: 采用坐标无关的公式化方法，尊重李群的底层几何结构，建立了相应的薛定谔系统，证明了其解的存在性和唯一性，并推导出几何控制器来最优地插值李群上的概率密度。

Result: 证明了相应薛定谔系统解的存在性和唯一性，构造性地推导出能够最优插值李群上概率密度的几何控制器，并在SO(2)和SO(3)上提供了数值示例验证结果。

Conclusion: 该工作成功开发了紧致连通李群上薛定谔桥问题的几何框架，避免了传统参数化的局限性，为李群上的概率密度最优插值提供了理论保证和实用控制器。

Abstract: This work studies the Schrödinger bridge problem for the kinematic equation on a compact connected Lie group. The objective is to steer a controlled diffusion between given initial and terminal densities supported over the Lie group while minimizing the control effort. We develop a coordinate-free formulation of this stochastic optimal control problem that respects the underlying geometric structure of the Lie group, thereby avoiding limitations associated with local parameterizations or embeddings in Euclidean spaces. We establish the existence and uniqueness of solution to the corresponding Schrödinger system. Our results are constructive in that they derive a geometric controller that optimally interpolates probability densities supported over the Lie group. To illustrate the results, we provide numerical examples on $\mathsf{SO}(2)$ and $\mathsf{SO}(3)$.

</details>


### [175] [Strong convergence, perturbation resilience and superiorization of Generalized Modular String-Averaging with infinitely many input operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14270)
*Kay Barshad,Yair Censor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了基于广义模块化字符串平均(GMSA)过程的迭代算法在无限输入算子序列下的强收敛性和有界扰动鲁棒性，应用于Hilbert空间中的可行性问题，并探讨了其在优越化方法和动态字符串平均中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解决无限多个输入算子情况下的可行性问题，扩展现有算法框架以包含模块化字符串平均选项，从而产生新的算法方案，同时保证强收敛性和有界扰动鲁棒性。

Method: 采用广义模块化字符串平均(GMSA)过程，在一般容许控制下处理无限序列的输入算子。方法包括一般情况下的强拟非扩张算子，以及特定的松弛严格非扩张算子子类用于建立有界扰动鲁棒性。还分析了优越化版本的主要算法行为。

Result: 证明了基于GMSA的迭代算法在无限输入算子序列下的强收敛性，建立了有界扰动鲁棒性，并将结果应用于优越化方法和动态字符串平均，展示了新算法方案的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅包含了多种早期算法作为特例，更重要的是引入了模块化字符串平均选项，产生了新的算法方案，特别适用于无限多个输入算子的情况。强收敛性保证以及在优越化和动态字符串平均中的应用是该研究的重要贡献。

Abstract: We study the strong convergence and bounded perturbation resilience of iterative algorithms based on the Generalized Modular String-Averaging (GMSA) procedure for infinite sequences of input operators under a general admissible control. These methods address a variety of feasibility-seeking problems in real Hilbert spaces, including the common fixed point problem and the convex feasibility problem. In addition to the general case, involving certain strongly quasi-nonexpansive input operators, we consider a specific subclass of their corresponding relaxed firmly nonexpansive operators. This subclass proves useful for establishing bounded perturbation resilience. We further demonstrate the applicability of our strong convergence results, within the GMSA framework, to the Superiorization Methodology and to Dynamic String-Averaging, analyzing the behavior of a superiorized version of our main algorithm. The novelty and significance of this work is that it not only includes a variety of earlier algorithms as special cases but, more importantly, it allows the use of modular options of string-averaging that give rise to new, hitherto unavailable, algorithmic schemes with emphasis on infinitely many input operators. The strong convergence guarantees and the applications for superiorization and dynamic string-averaging are also important facets.

</details>


### [176] [Second order necessary conditions for quantum stochastic optimal control problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14277)
*Penghui Wang,Shan Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文为量子随机系统中的最优控制建立了二阶必要条件，采用变分方法分析由费米子布朗运动驱动的量子随机微分方程系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子随机控制领域缺乏系统的二阶必要条件理论，需要建立类似经典随机控制中的变分方法，为量子系统的优化问题提供理论基础。

Method: 采用变分方法，类似于经典随机控制中的技术，分析由费米子布朗运动驱动的量子随机微分方程系统，其中控制同时出现在漂移项和扩散项中。

Result: 建立了量子随机系统最优控制的二阶必要条件，为量子随机控制中的优化问题提供了理论框架。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子随机控制领域的优化问题奠定了理论基础，为进一步的理论探索和实际应用提供了支持。

Abstract: This paper aims to establish second order necessary conditions for optimal control in quantum stochastic systems. We employ a variational approach, analogous to methods in classical stochastic control, to analyze systems governed by quantum stochastic differential equations driven by fermionic Brownian motion, where the control enters both the drift and diffusion terms. This result provides a theoretical foundation for further exploration of optimization problems and their practical applications in the field of quantum stochastic control.

</details>


### [177] [A Convergence-Guaranteed Algorithm for Stochastic Optimal Control Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14310)
*Mohsen Amidzadeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于Malliavin微积分的随机最优控制问题新方法，避免了传统伴随过程的需求，从而降低了计算复杂度并缓解了维度灾难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于随机最大值原理的迭代算法需要伴随敏感性分析，这要求模拟伴随过程（通常是后向随机微分方程），该过程必须同时适应前向滤波并满足终端条件，显著增加了计算复杂度并加剧了维度灾难问题。

Method: 开发了基于Malliavin微积分的随机最大值原理，设计了一种无需伴随过程的迭代算法。该算法需要Malliavin导数，但可以基于前向模拟器高效计算。

Result: 与标准迭代算法的实证比较表明，该方法缓解了维度瓶颈，同时在所考虑的随机最优控制问题上提供了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 基于Malliavin微积分的随机最大值原理为随机最优控制问题提供了一种有效的替代方法，避免了传统伴随过程的计算负担，在保持性能的同时显著降低了计算复杂度。

Abstract: Stochastic Optimal Control Problems (SOCPs) plays a major role in the sequential decision-making challenges. There exist various iterative algorithms, under framework of stochastic maximum principle, that sequentially find the optimal control decision. However, they are based on the adjoint sensitivity analysis that necessitates simulation of an adjoint process, typically a backward stochastic differential equation (SDE) that must simultaneously be adapted to a forward filtration and satisfy a terminal condition, which substantially increases complexity and exacerbates the curse of dimensionality. We instead develop a stochastic maximum principle based on the Malliavin calculus, which enables us to devise an iterative algorithm without need of an adjoint process. Our algorithm however needs the Malliavin derivative that can be efficiently computed based on a forward simulator. Empirical comparisons against standard iterative algorithms demonstrate that our approach alleviates the dimensionality bottleneck while delivering competitive performance on the considered SOCPs.

</details>


### [178] [Tractable bank capital structure: optimal control under Basel III constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14557)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Etienne Chevalier,Vathana Ly Vath,Yuqiong Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 银行在巴塞尔III约束下的最优风险管理：通过降维方法将复杂的资产负债表动态简化为可处理的一维杠杆率过程，得到简单可解释的股息支付和资本补充策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行需要在巴塞尔III严格的偿付能力和流动性约束下，同时优化风险投资、股息支付和资本结构，而昂贵的股权发行作为困境恢复工具。需要开发一个既能满足监管要求又能最大化股东价值的综合风险管理框架。

Method: 将问题表述为随机控制问题，通过降维技术将高维资产负债表动态简化为可处理的一维杠杆率过程，并包含状态相关的投资限制。推导出简单可解释的策略：在反射上界支付股息，在困境边界进行资本补充并跳跃到唯一目标水平。通过解析方法表征这些阈值，并分析其对监管参数的敏感性。

Result: 获得了简单的最优策略：股息支付在杠杆率上界进行，资本补充仅在困境边界发生并跳跃到特定目标水平。解析表征了阈值及其对监管参数的敏感性。通过蒙特卡洛模拟解决了外部优化问题，绘制了股东价值与生存概率之间的有效前沿，比较了有无杠杆上限的情况。

Conclusion: 收紧偿付能力要求通常能提供最佳的安全性与盈利性权衡。该方法为银行在巴塞尔III约束下的风险管理提供了简单可操作的策略框架，并为监管政策设计提供了量化分析工具。

Abstract: Banks must optimize risky investments, dividend payouts, and capital structure under tight Basel III solvency and liquidity constraints, while costly equity issuance serves as a distress-recovery tool. We formulate this as a stochastic control problem that reduces the high-dimensional balance-sheet dynamics to a tractable one-dimensional process in the leverage ratio, with state-dependent investment limits. The resulting policy is simple and interpretable: pay dividends at an upper reflection barrier and, when needed, recapitalize only at the distress boundary, jumping to a unique target level. We characterize these thresholds analytically and show their sensitivity to regulatory parameters. From a regulatory viewpoint, we solve an outer optimization problem that maps the efficient frontier between shareholder value and survival probability (via Monte Carlo), with and without leverage caps. Results highlight that tightening solvency requirements often yields the best safety-profitability trade-off.

</details>


### [179] [Online Learning for Supervisory Switching Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14762)
*Haoyuan Sun,Ali Jadbabaie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于部分观测线性动态系统的监督切换控制方法，结合多臂老虎机算法，在有限时间内识别最佳控制器并保证系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督控制缺乏有限时间性能保证，而现有非渐近方法需要系统稳定等限制性假设，无法测试可能不稳定的控制器。需要弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出数据驱动的监督控制算法，通过评分标准评估候选控制器，利用系统可观测性分离历史状态影响，既能检测不稳定控制器又能准确进行系统辨识。

Result: 提出两种算法变体，具有维度无关的有限时间保证，能在O(N log N)步内识别最适合的控制器，同时实现相对于系统扰动的有限L2增益。

Conclusion: 该研究为非渐近监督控制提供了理论框架，将多臂老虎机算法与控制系统理论结合，解决了传统方法在有限时间性能和稳定性保证方面的局限性。

Abstract: We study supervisory switching control for partially-observed linear dynamical systems. The objective is to identify and deploy the best controller for the unknown system by periodically selecting among a collection of $N$ candidate controllers, some of which may destabilize the underlying system. While classical estimator-based supervisory control guarantees asymptotic stability, it lacks quantitative finite-time performance bounds. Conversely, current non-asymptotic methods in both online learning and system identification require restrictive assumptions that are incompatible in a control setting, such as system stability, which preclude testing potentially unstable controllers. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel, non-asymptotic analysis of supervisory control that adapts multi-armed bandit algorithms to address these control-theoretic challenges. Our data-driven algorithm evaluates candidate controllers via scoring criteria that leverage system observability to isolate the effects of historical states, enabling both detection of destabilizing controllers and accurate system identification. We present two algorithmic variants with dimension-free, finite-time guarantees, where each identifies the most suitable controller in $\mathcal{O}(N \log N)$ steps, while simultaneously achieving finite $L_2$-gain with respect to system disturbances.

</details>


### [180] [Free Final Time Adaptive Mesh Covariance Steering via Sequential Convex Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14868)
*Joshua Pilipovsky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于非线性随机微分方程自由终端时间协方差控制的序列凸规划框架，该框架通过时间归一化和自适应时间网格优化同时优化控制策略和时间分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协方差控制方法通常固定终端时间，无法优化时间分配。对于具有加性和乘性扩散的非线性随机系统，需要在优化控制策略的同时优化时间网格，以提高协方差精度和性能。

Method: 1) 通过时间归一化处理自由终端时间目标，引入每区间时间膨胀变量实现自适应离散化网格；2) 推导精确的局部线性随机模型（保留乘性结构）；3) 提出可处理的离散化方法，保持相关扩散项；4) 通过锥/半定协方差控制松弛求解每个SCP子问题，包含终端矩约束和状态/控制机会约束。

Result: 数值实验验证了该方法在具有阻力和速度相关扩散的非线性双积分器上的有效性，展示了通过自适应时间分配实现自由终端时间最小化，以及相对于冻结扩散线性化方法的改进协方差精度。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了具有乘性噪声的非线性随机系统自由终端时间协方差控制问题，通过同时优化控制策略和时间网格分配，实现了更好的性能和协方差精度。

Abstract: In this paper we develop a sequential convex programming (SCP) framework for free-final-time covariance steering of nonlinear stochastic differential equations (SDEs) subject to both additive and multiplicative diffusion. We cast the free-final-time objective through a time-normalization and introduce per-interval time-dilation variables that induce an adaptive discretization mesh, enabling the simultaneous optimization of the control policy and the temporal grid. A central difficulty is that, under multiplicative noise, accurate covariance propagation within SCP requires retaining the first-order diffusion linearization and its coupling with time dilation. We therefore derive the exact local linear stochastic model (preserving the multiplicative structure) and introduce a tractable discretization that maintains the associated diffusion terms, after which each SCP subproblem is solved via conic/semidefinite covariance-steering relaxations with terminal moment constraints and state/control chance constraints. Numerical experiments on a nonlinear double-integrator with drag and velocity-dependent diffusion validate free-final-time minimization through adaptive time allocation and improved covariance accuracy relative to frozen-diffusion linearizations.

</details>


### [181] [The impact of machine learning forecasting on strategic decision-making for Bike Sharing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14901)
*Enrico Angelelli,Andrea Mor,Carlotta Orsenigo,M. Grazia Speranza,Carlo Vercellis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 使用机器学习预测共享单车站点借还车差异，结合仿真框架支持长期决策和日常运营优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决共享单车系统中站点间车辆分布不平衡问题，通过准确预测借还车差异来优化车辆调度和长期规划

Method: 采用机器学习技术预测各站点借还车差异，将预测结果整合到仿真框架中，用于模拟日常动态和车辆重新分配

Result: 基于意大利布雷西亚真实数据评估，机器学习预测优于替代方法，且能显著提升仿真框架输出质量

Conclusion: 机器学习预测结合仿真框架能有效支持共享单车系统的长期决策和日常运营优化，提高系统效率

Abstract: In this paper, machine learning techniques are used to forecast the difference between bike returns and withdrawals at each station of a bike sharing system. The forecasts are integrated into a simulation framework that is used to support long-term decisions and model the daily dynamics, including the relocation of bikes. We assess the quality of the machine learning-based forecasts in two ways. Firstly, we compare the forecasts with alternative prediction methods. Secondly, we analyze the impact of the forecasts on the quality of the output of the simulation framework. The evaluation is based on real-world data of the bike sharing system currently operating in Brescia, Italy.

</details>


### [182] [Optimal control of a tumor growth model with hyperbolic relaxation of the chemical potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14960)
*Pierluigi Colli,Elisabetta Rocca,Jürgen Sprekels*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有双曲松弛的Cahn-Hilliard型肿瘤生长相场模型的最优控制问题，涵盖正则和对数型双阱势，建立控制到状态算子的Fréchet可微性，推导一阶必要最优性条件，并基于此获得最优控制的稀疏性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究肿瘤生长相场模型的最优控制问题，该模型采用双曲松弛代替传统的抛物松弛化学势，旨在分析正则和对数型双阱势下的最优控制理论，为肿瘤生长控制提供数学基础。

Method: 在合适的Banach空间中建立控制到状态算子的Fréchet可微性，推导一阶必要最优性条件（变分不等式形式，包含伴随状态变量），并利用这些条件分析最优控制的稀疏性。

Result: 证明了控制到状态算子的Fréchet可微性，建立了包含伴随状态变量的一阶必要最优性条件（变分不等式），并推导出最优控制的稀疏性结果。

Conclusion: 成功建立了具有双曲松弛的Cahn-Hilliard型肿瘤生长相场模型的最优控制理论框架，涵盖正则和对数型双阱势，为肿瘤生长控制问题提供了完整的数学分析和稀疏性结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the optimal control of a phase field model for a tumor growth model of Cahn--Hilliard type in which the often assumed parabolic relaxation of the chemical potential is replaced by a hyperbolic one. Both the cases when the double-well potential governing the phase evolution is of either regular or logarithmic type are covered by the analysis. We show the Fréchet differentiability of the associated control-to-state operator in suitable Banach spaces and establish first-order necessary optimality conditions in terms of a variational inequality involving the adjoint state variables. The necessary optimality conditions are then used to derive sparsity results for the optimal controls.

</details>


### [183] [Riemannian Optimization Framework on the Generalized Quaternionic Stiefel Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15111)
*Hiroyuki Sato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文引入了广义四元数Stiefel流形，研究了其在黎曼优化中的几何性质，推导了相关几何量的显式表达式，并将广义四元数特征值问题表述为该流形上的优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究广义四元数Stiefel流形的几何结构，为在四元数域上进行黎曼优化提供理论基础，特别是解决广义四元数特征值问题等应用。

Method: 将广义四元数Stiefel流形视为实黎曼流形，推导了切空间、法空间、切空间正交投影、回缩映射和向量传输的显式表达式，并应用黎曼优化方法求解广义四元数特征值问题。

Result: 建立了广义四元数Stiefel流形的完整几何框架，获得了所有关键几何量的显式表达式，并通过数值实验验证了所提框架在求解广义四元数特征值问题中的可行性。

Conclusion: 成功构建了广义四元数Stiefel流形的几何理论，为四元数域上的黎曼优化提供了有效工具，特别是在处理广义四元数特征值问题方面展示了实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces the generalized quaternionic Stiefel manifold and studies its geometry for Riemannian optimization. We clarify its relationships with existing manifolds, especially the real generalized Stiefel manifold and the quaternionic Stiefel manifold, and derive explicit expressions for several geometric quantities on the proposed manifold. In particular, the generalized quaternionic Stiefel manifold is regarded as a real Riemannian manifold, and expressions for the tangent space, normal space, the orthogonal projection onto the tangent space, a retraction, and a vector transport on this manifold are derived. As an application, the generalized quaternionic eigenvalue problem is formulated as an optimization problem on this manifold, and a numerical example is solved by Riemannian optimization methods to demonstrate the viability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [184] [A Consensus-based optimization algorithm using Gaussian processes for global optimization problems in Sobolev spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15337)
*Mahmoud Khatab,Claudia Totzeck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于高斯过程的无限维全局优化算法，用于求解Sobolev空间中的优化问题，适用于带边界条件和状态约束的非线性边值问题及最优控制问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有限维共识算法推广到无限维Sobolev空间，解决带约束的全局优化问题，特别是非线性边值问题和最优控制问题

Method: 利用高斯过程从Sobolev空间采样满足边界条件和状态约束的函数，结合共识算法思想，通过高斯过程的边缘化特性实现无限维算法的离散化

Result: 算法在非线性边值问题和受常微分方程组约束的非线性最优控制问题上表现出可行性，数值结果验证了其性能

Conclusion: 基于高斯过程的无限维共识算法能有效处理Sobolev空间中的全局优化问题，特别适用于带约束的非线性边值问题和最优控制问题

Abstract: We propose an algorithm to approximate solutions of global optimization problems in Sobolev spaces that follows the spirit of Consensus-based algorithms in finite dimensions. The main ingredient are Gaussian processes. In fact, we exploit their rich toolbox in order to draw sample functions from Sobolev spaces that satisfy initial values, boundary conditions or state constraints. Well-known marginalization properties of Gaussian processes help us to discretize the algorithm, that is stated in infinite dimensions, appropriately. We illustrate the performance of the algorithm and show its feasibility for nonlinear boundary value problems with state constraints as well as nonlinear optimal control problems constrained by a system of ordinary differential equations with several numerical results.

</details>


### [185] [A superposition approach for the ISS Lyapunov-Krasovskii theorem with pointwise dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15346)
*Andrii Mironchenko,Fabian Wirth,Antoine Chaillet,Lucas Brivadis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文证明：对于时滞系统，只要存在具有逐点耗散性的Lyapunov-Krasovskii泛函，且该泛函能保证一致全局稳定性，就足以确保输入到状态稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稳定性分析通常基于经典范数评估解的行为，但这种方法可能无法提供足够紧的估计。本文旨在开发一种新的稳定性理论，通过特定的Lyapunov-Krasovskii泛函来评估解的行为，从而获得更精确的稳定性条件。

Method: 开发了一种基于Lyapunov-Krasovskii泛函的稳定性理论，其中解的行为不是通过经典范数评估，而是通过特定的LKF来评估。证明了只要存在具有逐点耗散性的LKF，并且同一LKF能保证一致全局稳定性，就足以确保输入到状态稳定性。

Result: 建立了新的稳定性判据：对于时滞系统，具有逐点耗散性的Lyapunov-Krasovskii泛函结合一致全局稳定性条件，足以保证输入到状态稳定性。通过示例说明了该方法相比传统范数方法的优势。

Conclusion: 提出的方法通过使用Lyapunov-Krasovskii泛函直接评估解的行为，提供了比传统范数方法更紧的稳定性估计，为时滞系统的输入到状态稳定性分析提供了更有效的工具。

Abstract: We show that the existence of a Lyapunov-Krasovskii functional (LKF) with pointwise dissipation (i.e. dissipation in terms of the current solution norm) suffices for input-to-state stability, provided that uniform global stability can also be ensured using the same LKF. To this end, we develop a stability theory, in which the behavior of solutions is not assessed through the classical norm but rather through a specific LKF, which may provide significantly tighter estimates. We discuss the advantages of our approach by means of an example.

</details>


### [186] [Mitigating Renewable-Induced Risks for Green and Conventional Ammonia Producers through Coordinated Production and Futures Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15360)
*Huayan Geng,Yangjun Zeng,Yiwei Qiu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为"可再生氨期货"的金融工具，用于对冲可再生能源制氨（ReP2A）生产中的波动风险，通过博弈论框架协调ReP2A生产商与传统灰氨（GA）生产商的生产和交易策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源制氨（ReP2A）使用可再生电力生产的氢气作为原料，是实现能源、交通和化工部门脱碳的有前景途径。然而，可再生能源发电的波动性导致氢气供应和氨生产不稳定，给ReP2A生产商和传统化石基灰氨（GA）生产商带来收入风险。现有研究主要依赖生产调度等工程措施管理风险，但效果受物理系统限制约束。

Method: 提出可再生氨期货金融工具，并将其与生产决策集成以对冲氨产量风险。为ReP2A和GA生产商开发生产和交易模型，使用条件风险价值（CVaR）表示不确定性下的风险偏好。建立博弈论框架，两个生产商在耦合的氨现货和期货市场中互动，采用纳什议价机制协调其生产和交易策略。

Result: 基于真实系统的案例研究表明，引入可再生氨期货使ReP2A和GA生产商的CVaR效用分别提高了5.103%和10.14%，显著改善了可再生能源不确定性下的利润稳定性。敏感性分析进一步证实了该机制在不同可再生能源波动水平和容量配置下的有效性。

Conclusion: 可再生氨期货是一种有效的金融工具，能够帮助ReP2A和GA生产商对冲可再生能源波动带来的风险，提高利润稳定性。该机制通过博弈论框架协调不同生产商利益，为可再生能源制氨市场的风险管理提供了创新解决方案。

Abstract: Renewable power-to-ammonia (ReP2A), which uses hydrogen produced from renewable electricity as feedstock, is a promising pathway for decarbonizing the energy, transportation, and chemical sectors. However, variability in renewable generation causes fluctuations in hydrogen supply and ammonia production, leading to revenue instability for both ReP2A producers and conventional fossil-based gray ammonia (GA) producers in the market. Existing studies mainly rely on engineering measures, such as production scheduling, to manage this risk, but their effectiveness is constrained by physical system limits. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a financial instrument termed \emph{renewable ammonia futures} and integrates it with production decisions to hedge ammonia output risk. Production and trading models are developed for both ReP2A and GA producers, with conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) used to represent risk preferences under uncertainty. A game-theoretic framework is established in which the two producers interact in coupled ammonia spot and futures markets, and a Nash bargaining mechanism coordinates their production and trading strategies. Case studies based on a real-world system show that introducing renewable ammonia futures increases the CVaR utilities of ReP2A and GA producers by 5.103% and 10.14%, respectively, improving profit stability under renewable uncertainty. Sensitivity analysis further confirms the effectiveness of the mechanism under different levels of renewable variability and capacity configurations.

</details>


### [187] [Unimodal self-oscillations and their sign-symmetry for discrete-time relay feedback systems with dead zone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15393)
*Kang Tong,Christian Grussler,Michelle S. Chong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有非负死区的离散时间线性时不变继电器反馈系统中单峰自激振荡的存在性条件，建立了基于全正性理论的解析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究离散时间LTI继电器反馈系统中单峰自激振荡的存在性，这类系统在控制工程中具有重要应用，但现有理论对具有死区的系统分析不足。

Method: 基于全正性理论建立新的解析框架，假设线性部分稳定且具有严格单调递减的无限支撑脉冲响应，通过分析环路增益的正负值数量关系来研究自激振荡。

Result: 证明了单峰自激振荡仅在环路增益的正负值数量在给定正周期内相等时存在（符号对称性），建立了存在条件、周期紧界，并解决了唯一性问题。

Conclusion: 为具有非负死区的离散时间LTI继电器反馈系统提供了单峰自激振荡的完整理论框架，包括存在性、周期界限和唯一性条件，扩展了现有振荡理论。

Abstract: This paper characterizes self-oscillations in discrete-time linear time-invariant (LTI) relay feedback systems with nonnegative dead zone. Specifically, we aim to establish existence criteria for unimodal self-oscillations, defined as periodic solutions where the output exhibits a single-peaked period. Assuming that the linear part of system is stable, with a strictly monotonically decreasing impulse response on its infinite support, we propose a novel analytical framework based on the theory of total positivity to address this problem. We demonstrate that unimodal self-oscillations subject to mild variation-based constraints exist only if the number of positive and negative values of the system's loop gain coincides within a given strictly positive period, i.e., the self-oscillation is sign-symmetric. Building upon these findings, we derive conditions for the existence of such self-oscillations, establish tight bounds on their periods, and address the question of their uniqueness.

</details>


### [188] [Minimal enclosing balls via geodesics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15488)
*Ariel Goodwin,Adrian S. Lewis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了对测地线空间中最小包围球问题的简化复杂度分析，改进了收敛率，并首次给出了曲率有上界的测地线空间的复杂度结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最小包围球算法多为几何方法，本文旨在突出其效率背后的度量要素。先前基于次梯度的研究在非正曲率测地空间中证明了复杂度结果，但分析复杂，需要更直观、自包含的分析方法

Method: 采用基于测地线的简单方法，在非正曲率测地空间中提供更简单、直观且自包含的复杂度分析，并首次将分析扩展到曲率有上界的测地空间

Result: 改进了非正曲率测地空间中算法的收敛率，并首次获得了曲率有上界的测地空间的复杂度结果

Conclusion: 通过简化分析框架，不仅改进了非正曲率测地空间中的收敛率，还成功将复杂度分析扩展到更一般的曲率有上界的测地空间，为最小包围球问题的度量分析提供了更全面的理论支持

Abstract: Algorithms for minimal enclosing ball problems are often geometric in nature. To highlight the metric ingredients underlying their efficiency, we focus here on a particularly simple geodesic-based method. A recent subgradient-based study proved a complexity result for this method in the broad setting of geodesic spaces of nonpositive curvature. We present a simpler, intuitive and self-contained complexity analysis in that setting, which also improves the convergence rate. We furthermore derive the first complexity result for the algorithm on geodesic spaces with curvature bounded above.

</details>


### [189] [A practical randomized trust-region method to escape saddle points in high dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15494)
*Radu-Alexandru Dragomir,Xiaowen Jiang,Bonan Sun,Nicolas Boumal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种改进的随机化信任域方法，通过随机化截断共轭梯度初始化，在鞍点附近放大随机性以逃逸，在局部极小值附近吸收随机性以收敛，解决了传统扰动梯度下降无法收敛的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扰动梯度下降(PGD)虽然能逃逸鞍点，但无法收敛且需要精确调参。需要一种既能逃逸鞍点又能收敛到局部极小值的实用算法。

Method: 改进经典的信任域-截断共轭梯度(TR-tCG)方法：随机化tCG子问题求解器的初始化，利用tCG在鞍点附近自动放大随机性以逃逸，在局部极小值附近吸收随机性以收敛。

Result: 提出的算法实用性强，仅需三个微小修改和一个新超参数(噪声尺度σ)。实现了鞍点逃逸和局部收敛的平衡，提供了实现和数值实验验证。

Conclusion: 通过随机化经典信任域方法，实现了既能逃逸鞍点又能收敛的实用算法，解决了PGD的局限性，为高维非凸优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Without randomization, escaping the saddle points of $f \colon \mathbb{R}^d \to \mathbb{R}$ requires at least $Ω(d)$ pieces of information about $f$ (values, gradients, Hessian-vector products). With randomization, this can be reduced to a polylogarithmic dependence in $d$. The prototypical algorithm to that effect is perturbed gradient descent (PGD): through sustained jitter, it reliably escapes strict saddle points. However, it also never settles: there is no convergence. What is more, PGD requires precise tuning based on Lipschitz constants and a preset target accuracy.
  To improve on this, we modify the time-tested trust-region method with truncated conjugate gradients (TR-tCG). Specifically, we randomize the initialization of tCG (the subproblem solver), and we prove that tCG automatically amplifies the randomization near saddles (to escape) and absorbs it near local minimizers (to converge). Saddle escape happens over several iterations. Accordingly, our analysis is multi-step, with several novelties.
  The proposed algorithm is practical: it essentially tracks the good behavior of TR-tCG, with three minute modifications and a single new hyperparameter (the noise scale $σ$). We provide an implementation and numerical experiments.

</details>


### [190] [A Technical Note on the Implementation and Use of PDCS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15504)
*Zhenwei Lin,Zikai Xiong,Dongdong Ge,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: PDCS是一个用于大规模锥优化问题的原始-对偶一阶求解器，基于重启原始-对偶混合梯度方法框架，包含自适应步长选择、自适应反射Halpern迭代、自适应重启和对角预条件等技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个高效的大规模锥优化求解器，解决传统二阶方法在处理大规模问题时的计算瓶颈，提供开源实现以促进研究和应用。

Method: 采用重启原始-对偶混合梯度方法框架，结合自适应步长选择、自适应反射Halpern迭代加速收敛、自适应重启策略防止振荡，以及对角预条件技术改善条件数。

Result: 实现了PDCS求解器，提供JuMP和CVXPY接口，包含完整的算法实现和用户指南，以Apache 2.0许可证开源发布在GitHub上。

Conclusion: PDCS为大规模锥优化问题提供了一个高效的一阶求解器，通过多种自适应技术实现了良好的收敛性能，开源实现便于研究和实际应用。

Abstract: This technical note documents the implementation and use of the Primal-Dual Conic Programming Solver (PDCS), a first-order solver for large-scale conic optimization problems introduced by Lin et al. (arXiv:2505.00311). It describes the algorithmic and implementation details underlying PDCS, including the restarted primal-dual hybrid gradient method framework, adaptive step-size selection, adaptive reflected Halpern iterations, adaptive restarts, and diagonal preconditioning. It also provides practical instructions for using PDCS, including its interfaces with JuMP and CVXPY, solver options, and illustrative code examples. PDCS is available at https://github.com/ZikaiXiong/PDCS under the Apache License 2.0.

</details>


### [191] [Notes on the primal-dual algorithm for convex optimization applied to X-ray tomographic image reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15535)
*Emil Y. Sidky,Xiaochuan Pan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 为成像科学家提供Chambolle-Pock原始对偶算法的背景知识，重点关注直觉理解和易于预处理的算法系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助成像科学家理解Chambolle和Pock的原始对偶算法，提供直观解释和易于预处理的算法框架

Method: 采用自包含的文档形式，提供非光滑凸分析的基本事实背景，重点关注算法直觉和预处理友好的系统

Result: 创建了专门针对成像科学家的算法解释材料，提供了直观理解和预处理方法

Conclusion: 成功为成像科学家提供了理解Chambolle-Pock原始对偶算法的背景知识，特别强调了直觉理解和预处理能力

Abstract: The purpose of these notes is to provide background on understanding the primal-dual algorithm of Chambolle and Pock [1] for imaging scientists. The presentation focuses on providing intuition and an algorithmic system that is amenable to pre-conditioning. The document aims to be self-contained, providing background on the essential facts of non-smooth convex analysis.[2]

</details>


### [192] [Saddle Point Evasion via Curvature-Regularized Gradient Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15606)
*Liraz Mudrik,Isaac Kaminer,Sean Kragelund,Abram H. Clark*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出Curvature-Regularized Gradient Dynamics (CRGD)方法，通过惩罚最负的Hessian特征值来增强目标函数，从而确定性地逃离鞍点并实现可控收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸优化在现代机器学习和控制任务中普遍存在，鞍点是高维设置中可靠收敛的主要障碍。梯度下降法无法感知曲率，随机扰动方法缺乏确定性保证，而牛顿类方法则受限于Hessian矩阵奇异性，因此需要一种能够确定性地、以可控速率逃离鞍点的方法。

Method: 提出曲率正则化梯度动力学(CRGD)方法，通过在目标函数中添加对最负Hessian特征值的平滑惩罚项，构建一个增强成本函数，该函数可作为优化Lyapunov函数，具有用户可选择的收敛速率，能够收敛到二阶稳定点。

Result: 在非凸矩阵分解示例上的数值实验证实，CRGD在所有测试配置下都能逃离鞍点，且逃离时间随特征值间隙减小而减少，这与梯度下降法的逃离时间随间隙减小而增加形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: CRGD提供了一种确定性地逃离鞍点的新方法，通过曲率正则化实现了可控收敛速率，为高维非凸优化问题提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Nonconvex optimization underlies many modern machine learning and control tasks, where saddle points pose the dominant obstacle to reliable convergence in high-dimensional settings. Escaping these saddle points deterministically and at a controllable rate remains an open challenge: gradient descent is blind to curvature, stochastic perturbation methods lack deterministic guarantees, and Newton-type approaches suffer from Hessian singularity. We present Curvature-Regularized Gradient Dynamics (CRGD), which augments the objective with a smooth penalty on the most negative Hessian eigenvalue, yielding an augmented cost that serves as an optimization Lyapunov function with user-selectable convergence rates to second-order stationary points. Numerical experiments on a nonconvex matrix factorization example confirm that CRGD escapes saddle points across all tested configurations, with escape time that decreases with the eigenvalue gap, in contrast to gradient descent, whose escape time grows inversely with the gap.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Benchmarking Zero-Shot Reasoning Approaches for Error Detection in Solidity Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13239)
*Eduardo Sardenberg,Antonio José Grandson Busson,Daniel de Sousa Moraes,Sérgio Colcher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 评估大型语言模型在Solidity智能合约漏洞检测中的表现，比较不同提示策略（零样本、零样本思维链、零样本思维树）在错误检测和错误分类任务上的效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约在区块链系统中扮演核心角色，但其安全漏洞可能导致重大财务损失和信任危机。虽然LLMs为自动化漏洞检测提供了新机会，但不同提示策略和模型选择在实际场景中的有效性仍不确定

Method: 使用包含400个合约的平衡数据集，评估最先进的LLMs在Solidity智能合约分析中的表现。设置两个任务：(1)错误检测（二元分类判断合约是否易受攻击），(2)错误分类（将预测问题分配到特定漏洞类别）。采用三种零样本提示策略：零样本、零样本思维链和零样本思维树

Result: 在错误检测任务中，CoT和ToT显著提高了召回率（通常接近95-99%），但通常降低了精确度，表明决策机制更敏感但产生更多误报。在错误分类任务中，Claude 3 Opus在ToT提示下获得最佳加权F1分数（90.8），其CoT表现紧随其后

Conclusion: 思维链和思维树提示策略能显著提高LLMs在智能合约漏洞检测中的召回率，但以精确度为代价。Claude 3 Opus在错误分类任务中表现最佳，展示了不同提示策略对模型性能的重要影响

Abstract: Smart contracts play a central role in blockchain systems by encoding financial and operational logic. Still, their susceptibility to subtle security flaws poses significant risks of financial loss and erosion of trust. LLMs create new opportunities for automating vulnerability detection, yet the effectiveness of different prompting strategies and model choices in real-world contexts remains uncertain. This paper evaluates state-of-the-art LLMs on Solidity smart contract analysis using a balanced dataset of 400 contracts under two tasks: (i) Error Detection, where the model performs binary classification to decide whether a contract is vulnerable, and (ii) Error Classification, where the model must assign the predicted issue to a specific vulnerability category. Models are evaluated using zero-shot prompting strategies, including zero-shot, zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT), and zero-shot Tree-of-Thought (ToT). In the Error Detection task, CoT and ToT substantially increase recall (often approaching $\approx 95$--$99\%$), but typically reduce precision, indicating a more sensitive decision regime with more false positives. In the Error Classification task, Claude 3 Opus attains the best Weighted F1-score (90.8) under the ToT prompt, followed closely by its CoT.

</details>


### [194] [Automating Document Intelligence in Statutory City Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13245)
*Lars Malmqvist,Robin Barber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发集成AI系统，自动化处理英国规划文件中的个人信息识别与编辑、元数据提取和图纸分析，采用AI-in-the-Loop设计，通过人工审核确保合规性，目前正在四个地方当局试点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决英国规划部门面临的立法冲突：《规划法》要求公开申请文件，而《数据保护法》要求保护个人信息。这种冲突导致大量手动工作负担，规划官员需处理大量文件，既耗费时间又存在法律合规风险。

Method: 开发集成AI系统，采用AI-in-the-Loop设计：1) 自动识别和编辑个人信息；2) 从规划文件中提取关键元数据；3) 分析建筑图纸中的指定特征。所有AI建议都需规划官员在现有软件中审核确认，无人工批准不执行任何操作。系统通过主动学习优先机制从人工监督中持续改进。

Result: 系统正在四个多样化的英国地方当局进行试点。论文详细描述了系统设计、AI2L工作流程和试点评估框架，并开发了初步投资回报模型以量化潜在节省并确保合作伙伴参与。

Conclusion: 该研究为在公共部门环境中部署AI以减少行政负担和管理合规风险提供了案例研究，展示了AI-in-the-Loop设计如何平衡自动化效率与人工监督需求，为类似公共部门应用提供了参考框架。

Abstract: UK planning authorities face a legislative conflict between the Planning Act, which mandates public access to application documents, and the Data Protection Act, which requires protection of personal information. This situation creates a manually intensive workload for processing large document volumes, diverting planning officers to administrative tasks and creating legal compliance risks. This paper presents an integrated AI system designed to address these challenges. The system automates the identification and redaction of personal information, extracts key metadata from planning documents, and analyzes architectural drawings for specified features. It operates with an AI-in-the-Loop (AI2L) design, presenting all suggestions for review and confirmation by planning officers directly within their existing software; no action is committed without explicit human approval. The system is designed to improve its performance over time by learning from this human oversight through active learning prioritization rather than autoapproval. The system is currently being piloted at four diverse UK local authorities. The paper details the system design, the AI2L workflow, and the evaluation framework used in the pilot. Additionally, it describes a preliminary Return on Investment (ROI) model developed to quantify potential savings and secure partner participation. This work provides a case study on deploying AI to reduce administrative burden and manage compliance risk in a public sector environment.

</details>
